SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE TABLE [dbo].[activities](
	[activity_id] [uniqueidentifier] NOT NULL,
	[activity_type] [varchar](15) NOT NULL,
	[form_number] [nvarchar](30) NOT NULL,
	[site_id] [int] NOT NULL,
	[unit_id] [uniqueidentifier] NULL,
	[started_at] [datetime2](0) NOT NULL,
	[finished_at] [datetime2](0) NOT NULL,
	[time_zone_id] [varchar](50) NOT NULL,
	[start_utc_offset_minutes] [smallint] NOT NULL,
	[finish_utc_offset_minutes] [smallint] NOT NULL,
	[started_at_local]  AS (dateadd(minute,[start_utc_offset_minutes],[started_at])) PERSISTED NOT NULL,
	[finished_at_local]  AS (dateadd(minute,[finish_utc_offset_minutes],[finished_at])) PERSISTED NOT NULL,
	[duration_minutes] [int] NOT NULL,
	[shift] [varchar](10) NULL,
	[technician_user_id] [uniqueidentifier] NULL,
	[technician_name] [nvarchar](120) NULL,
	[technician_nrp] [nvarchar](30) NULL,
	[group_leader] [nvarchar](120) NULL,
	[remarks] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
	[created_at] [datetime2](0) NOT NULL,
	[created_by] [uniqueidentifier] NULL,
	[updated_at] [datetime2](0) NULL,
	[updated_by] [uniqueidentifier] NULL,
	[row_version] [timestamp] NOT NULL,
 CONSTRAINT [PK_activities] PRIMARY KEY CLUSTERED 
(
	[activity_id] ASC
)WITH (PAD_INDEX = OFF, STATISTICS_NORECOMPUTE = OFF, IGNORE_DUP_KEY = OFF, ALLOW_ROW_LOCKS = ON, ALLOW_PAGE_LOCKS = ON, OPTIMIZE_FOR_SEQUENTIAL_KEY = OFF) ON [PRIMARY]
) ON [PRIMARY]
GO

/****** Object:  Trigger [dbo].[TR_activities_site_zone_immutable]    Script Date: 17/11/2026 10:50:25 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE TRIGGER [dbo].[TR_activities_site_zone_immutable]
ON [dbo].[activities]
AFTER UPDATE
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    IF (UPDATE(site_id) OR UPDATE(time_zone_id))
       AND EXISTS (
            SELECT 1
            FROM inserted AS i
            INNER JOIN deleted AS d ON d.activity_id = i.activity_id
            WHERE i.site_id <> d.site_id
               OR i.time_zone_id <> d.time_zone_id
       )
        THROW 50010, N'activities.site_id and activities.time_zone_id cannot be changed after creation.', 1;
END
GO
ALTER TABLE [dbo].[activities] ENABLE TRIGGER [TR_activities_site_zone_immutable]
GO

CREATE TRIGGER [dbo].[TR_audit_log_block_delete]
ON [dbo].[audit_log]
INSTEAD OF DELETE
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    THROW 50021, N'audit_log is append-only: DELETE is not allowed.', 1;
END
GO
ALTER TABLE [dbo].[audit_log] ENABLE TRIGGER [TR_audit_log_block_delete]
GO
